# `pcloud-daemon`

**Maturity:** Evolving product surface

**Version:** `0.1.0`

**Directory:** `crates/pcloud-daemon`

**Manifest:** [`crates/pcloud-daemon/Cargo.toml`](https://github.com/ezechiel203/pcloud-rs/blob/main/crates/pcloud-daemon/Cargo.toml)

Long-running pcloud-rs daemon (pcloudd): runtime orchestration, backends, IPC server, vault.

## Feature-family profile

**Why it exists.** Own long-lived credentials, validated request dispatch, state, background work, and native resources in one per-user composition root.

**What it is good for.** Running pcloudd as the authoritative local service behind CLI, SDK, web, sync, mount, backup, health, and metrics.

**Why it is good at that job.** A single RuntimeShell, peer-authenticated IPC, durable stores, controlled shutdown, and narrowly composed backends centralize authority and cleanup.
